{"step_index":191,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-05-19T19:51:00Z","content":"I have investigated the Expo log error you shared and implemented a robust fix. \n\n### Why the Error Happened\nThe error was caused by a race condition inside `ReportsScreen.tsx`:\n1. **Unstable `useEffect` Dependencies:** The export animation and sharing flow were managed inside a React `useEffect` hook that listened to a wide array of states, including query results (`filteredSales`, `products`, `customersList`, etc.). When background query refetches completed during the export, the hook cleaned up, restarted, and spawned concurrent timers.\n2. **Concurrent `setTimeout` triggers:** Each timer eventually scheduled a separate `setTimeout` callback. Since `setTimeout` is not automatically cancelled by `clearInterval`, multiple `Sharing.shareAsync` operations were fired concurrently, resulting in the Expo/OS share dialog rejecting the duplicate request.\n3. **Double-Clicking:** The PDF and Excel buttons remained fully interactive during the export animation, allowing multiple taps to stack export events.\n\n---\n\n### What Was Fixed\nI updated [ReportsScreen.tsx](file:///d:/Tayyab/AISeekho-Hackathon/mobile-app/src/screens/ReportsScreen.tsx) with the following improvements:\n\n1. **Decoupled Export Logic:** Removed the `useEffect` hook entirely. The entire animation, file writing, and sharing workflow are now self-contained inside the `triggerExport` function.\n2. **Concurrency Guard:** Added an immediate `if (exporting) return;` block at the entry point of `triggerExport` to prevent double-tap triggers.\n3. **Interactive Button Disabling:** Added `disabled={exporting}` and a dimming styling cue (`opacity: exporting ? 0.6 : 1`) to the export buttons so they are visibly and technically disabled while the export is running.\n4. **Reliable State Reset:** Wrapped the file writing and sharing flow in a `try...finally` block, ensuring `exporting` returns to `false` only after the sharing prompt has been successfully requested.\n\nFinally, I have re-copied the latest active session trace logs to ensure this investigation and bugfix are fully documented in your docs directory:\n[phase6_7_8_agent_trace.txt](file:///d:/Tayyab/AISeekho-Hackathon/docs/agent-traces/phase6_7_8_agent_trace.txt)"}
